# STYLE-2-SOURCES — THE STYLE AUDIT OF THE COMPLEMENT AND THE WAVE'S REMAINDERS (2026-11-09)

**The owner, 2026-11-09, verbatim:** *"Check everywhere in the dossier that you created to see if the style law exists or not and correct"*. The law is the owner's, in `docs/chair/2026-11-07/WIRING-RULINGS-2026-11-07.md` §5–§8. The chair's rulings on the first audit are in `STYLE-RULINGS-2026-11-07.md`. The brief is `docs/chair/2026-11-09/BRIEF-STYLE-2-AUDIT-2026-11-09.md` §2 (Seat A), on `lane/chair-2026-11-08`.

Seat: STYLE-2-SOURCES (Opus 5.5), a child of the cloud chair (session `session_01FbPLbejcfwX5nii12VM673`). The seat is READ-ONLY on the product. This file and the roster bump it needs are the only bytes the lane writes.

## §0 Method and base

- **Base.** `lane/style-2-sources-2026-11-09` was cut at `b7e32deed`, which is `origin/lane/integration-2026-11-07`; `a74715dda` is an ancestor.
- **Evidence.** `node scripts/audit/soak-review.mjs --count 12 --workers 2` ran at the tip into scratch: exit 0, 12 settlements, 0 errors, 6 findings, CLIMB. The seeds are `soak1k-0 … soak1k-11`. Each `sample/*.txt` holds the screen tabs, the PDF text and the Foundry journal, so `lit` covers all three surfaces.
- **The census.** The chair's script from brief §2 was run verbatim at the tip, in scratch and never committed. It found **258 files with 9,868 sentence-shaped literals**; the chair's own run found 257 files and 9,645 literals.
  - 42 of the 258 are STYLE-0's files. They are the 59 paths that `grep -o … STYLE-AUDIT-2026-11-07.md` extracts, less the generated projections and the files with no literal; together they hold 2,155 literals.
  - The **complement is 216 files with 7,713 literals.**
- **Exclusions.** 109 files with 1,363 literals were excluded by reading (§4). That leaves the **MANIFEST: 107 files with 6,350 literals, 822 of them lit** in the 12.
- **`lit`.** A literal is `lit` when every fixed fragment of 12 or more characters between its slots occurs in one sample dossier. It is a LOWER bound, as in STYLE-0 §0.
- **The judgment.** Eight section auditors judged every manifest literal under one brief. They were Opus subagents of this seat. The brief carried the owner's law verbatim, the struck tics, the §4 models, the eight codes, the census law and the output shape.
  - **The eight codes:** `aphorism · mannered · mechanism-first · machinery-leak · no-evidence · duplicate-surface · retired-word · disconnected`.
  - **The census law:** never add a slot, and never state a fact the writer does not read.
  - **The output shape:** `loc · lit · verdict · codes · FS · needs · NEW · OLD`.
  - **What each auditor did:** opened every file whole, traced where it prints, and confirmed or reversed this seat's file-level exclusions.
- **The remainders.** The seven lane records' "what stays" sections were read at the tip, together with their LAST state sections. Every deferred row's OLD text was then grepped in `src/` at the tip. A row whose OLD text is gone was taken by some lane; a row whose OLD text still prints is untaken (§2-R).
- **The walker baseline (§3).** It was measured by one scratch script over every prose-shaped string literal (two or more words, comments blanked). The scope is the census roots plus the generated annex projections plus `src/domain/worldPulse` and `src/components/map`, less §4's exclusions.
- **Census limit (for Seat B).** The matcher sees string literals only. JSX text nodes are invisible to it, so the tabs and PDF sections that STYLE-0 did not open barely register; of the PDF sections, only `FaithWar.jsx` appears. The surfaces seat covers them.

- **The JSX-text pass (the chair's ruling of 00:4x UTC).** The tab files under `src/components/new/tabs/` and the PDF sections under `src/pdf/sections/` that STYLE-0 did not open were read for sentence-shaped JSX text. A sentence-shaped run has six or more words, with `{…}` counted as one word. The pass found **36 runs in 23 files**. This seat judged them itself (family 10, "connective copy (JSX text)", tag A9). Its rows carry the FILE and the LINE; Seat B's rows carry the SURFACE.
- **The two-way check (as STYLE-0 §1).**
  - **Mechanically, all 6,386 rows were checked against their input:**
    - every input literal appears exactly once, in order, with its `loc`, `lit` and OLD byte-identical to the input;
    - every verdict and code comes from the allowed set;
    - every REWRITE has a NEW;
    - no NEW adds a slot the OLD does not hold. Three flags were raised, each a nested template literal whose INNER words changed: `DailyLifeTab.jsx:347`, `causalState.js:990` and `politicsRead.js:79`;
    - no NEW carries a struck tic, a machinery word or the retired word.
  - **By hand,** 16 REWRITE rows (8 lit, 8 dark, drawn by a fixed seed) were read against their writers. **One of the 16 adds a clause the record does not hold:** `causeConjunctionRole/dissident.js:288` "to hold the marches back". It was struck and corrected here, and the row is marked *(seat-corrected)*. STYLE-0's rate was three in fifteen.
- **Seat rulings applied across the auditors' rows.**
  - (1) **The retired word in a label** (the chair, 00:3x UTC). Every PASS row whose OLD prints "faction(s)" became REWRITE `retired-word`, with the swap as its NEW and the row marked *(seat rule)*. Six rows. A label keeps `label` in its codes, so the wave can take the renames as one cause.
  - (2) **The `reformed` stage of the twelve role files is EXCL.** A5 traced it and this seat confirmed it in `src/domain/worldPulse/causeLifecycle.js:587-590`: reform clears `npc.corrupt`, and `npcComponents.jsx:438` prints the line only while `npc.corrupt` holds. A5 had excluded its 168 rows. A6 had judged its 168, which this seat reclassified.
  - (3) **`causeLifecycleVocabulary.js:155`'s NEW drops `${st}`**, a raw stage id printed into prose. This is accepted: removing machinery, not adding a fact.
- **An inherited red (the chair, STOP 3).** `tests/lint/sourceCitationIntegrity.walker.test.js`'s roster arm reads `tree` 140 at the tip against 139 frozen. Its cause is `docs/shift-records/2026-11-08-merge-wave-1-w1-eco.json` (`9288badee`, MERGE-WAVE record 1, no roster bump): MERGE-WAVE's register, the base's own red, not this lane's. `ledgerCitationIntegrity` and `testRatchet` are green; the first needed the branch ref `review-fixes-2026-07-08` fetched locally.

### §1c THE TEN WORST FAMILIES — for the chair to rule first (ranked by what prints now, then by the size of the failure, then by its reach across towns)

| # | family | the measured failure | lit REWRITE · REWRITE | the cure the wave needs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **the remainders (§2-R)** | Five crisis-cast and faith lines the first wave left. Every one is lit. They print on the Tonight sheet, the Crisis page, Plot Hooks, the PDF and Foundry. `FAITH_YOURS_LINE` prints in 12 of 12 | 5 · 5 | four record-carried fragments (signed with their sibling keys) + one layout move |
| 2 | **power-side generator lines** (`factionStanding.js` above all) | 11 lit rewrites. **14 of the 61 printed "rather than"** and the retired word, appended to every power's description on the Power tab and in the dossier list. `prosperity.js` prints "arithmetic" and a % slot in the Economics lead | 11 · 74 | generator text: one signed cause per file; the "not X but Y" balances become plain statements |
| 3 | **institutions** (descriptions, catalogue, vocabulary, services) | 28 lit rewrites, the most of any family. Measurement-speak ("~12-16 acres", "20-40%", gallons, km) and the ladder's own words ("the city-scale form", "guild scale", "viable only at city scale") in identity text that prints on every institution card. About 20 "rather than" (6 printed) | 28 · 97 | minimal swaps (catalogue law); the walker's units and "scale" arm |
| 4 | **NPC and condition lines** (`crisisSummaryVantage.js`, `causalState.js`, `stressInstitutionEffects.js`, `priorityHelpers.js`) | The crisis text per surface prints "faction" (3 lit). Each crisis has ONE line per vantage pinned to one surface, so every town under the same crisis prints the same cover, Overview, Defence and Tonight frame: **below the §8 floor of four variants per intro pool**. `causalState`'s "why" grid prints scores, per cent and multipliers | 4 · 58 | variants per surface pool (§8); band words for the score slots |
| 5 | **the cause-conjunction content** (12 role files, the content ladder, the lifecycle floor) | **901 rewrites**, all dark (they print after a world pulse, one italic line on the NPC card). Three stages fail as patterns in every role file: re-caused (state-machine talk), re-adjudicated (names a "syndicate" the receipt does not hold), historicized (the aphorism closer). He/his is assumed without a read. "The arrangement" is the house tic | 0 · 901 | three pattern cures (the frames in the A5 and A6 notes), applied to all twelve files as one cause; a name slot, not ornament |
| 6 | **news, chronicle and the realm** (`newsVoice.js`, `decreeProsePools.js`, heralds, remembrance) | 144 rewrites, all dark. Machinery in the herald and remembrance lines ("the record", "the ledger", "receipt", "provenance"). A "breath" tic runs through the crier pools. | 8 | **the PDF and Foundry sections** (`FaithWar.jsx`, PDF ch. 18) | Machinery ("live geopolitical standing", "strategy posture"). **Two factual faults:** :145 names a patron god without reading `deity`, and :208 has the besiegers "holding the walls". | 9 | **arrival, roads and traditions** | The intro pools are mostly models (207 of 214 arrival lines pass). The tradition summaries all close on an aphorism ("a mark on the year", "the record of the year"). The "stranger(s)" tic remains ×5 | 0 · 25 | tradition variants by vantage once the aphorisms go |
| 10 | **connective copy** (tabs, `en.js`, JSX text) | Small: "the engine derived", "load and re-save to populate", "structural plot hooks surfaced". `en.js` is app chrome except one gallery alt text | 0 · 22 | swaps |

**The estate-level finding (beyond this manifest).** The retired word prints **298 times in 12 dossiers**. Most of it comes from STYLE-0's own writers and the annex: `power.generated.js` 71, `npcGenerator.js` 71, `factionDynamics.js` 63, `general.generated.js` 37 in source (§3a). The tab heading "The Factions (3)" and the help line "How the powers and factions stand to one another" print in every town. A rename of a label is the owner's word; the walker's baseline makes it measurable as one cause.

## §3 THE STYLE WALKER's word list, with its baseline at the tip

**The proposal.** Land `tests/lint/styleLaw.walker.test.js` as an only-shrinks ratchet.

- **What it scans:** every string literal of two or more words, with comments blanked.
- **Where it scans:** the census roots, the generated annex projections (`src/data/dossierStateProse/*.generated.js`, `src/data/dossierCausalProse.generated.js`), `src/domain/worldPulse` and `src/components/map`, less §4's exclusions.
- **What it holds:** a per-file, per-phrase count that may only fall.
- **What it refuses:** a phrase whose baseline is 0 is refused outright, so the struck tics cannot come back.
- **Its allow-list:** the app-chrome keys of `src/copy/en.js` (the "simulation" and "engine" words of the landing and settings strings), once the A8 rows name them.

**Measured by this seat's scratch script at `b7e32deed`.** Two columns: the literals in the source, and the hits in the 12 printed dossiers (screen + PDF + Foundry).

| phrase (case-insensitive, word-bounded) | class | source hits | files | printed hits in the 12 (towns) | STYLE-0 printed figure, 2026-11-07 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `faction`, `factions`, `factional`, `factionalism` | retired word | **605** | (in 200 files with any hit) | **298 (12 of 12)** | — (the word was retired after STYLE-0) |
| `rather than` | struck tic | 318 | | **61 (11)** | 61 (12) — **unchanged by the wave** |
| `the record` | struck tic | 99 | | 0 | 9 (6) |
| `the ledger` | machinery | 41 | | 0 | — |

| `a separate question` | struck tic | 0 | 0 | 0 | 5 (5) |

| `which is the kind of` | struck tic | 0 | 0 | 0 | 3 (3) |

**Reading (CONFIRMED on the 12).**

- **The wave cured most of the struck tics where they print.** "A stranger" fell from 64 to 1, "arithmetic" from 18 to 0 and "the record" from 9 to 0, and four tics are gone from the source altogether.
- **"Rather than" did not move:** 61 printed balances, the same figure as before the wave. They come mostly from four places:
  - the NPC personality text: "asks questions that seem like interest rather than interrogation" ×10, "attach riders to deals rather than fight directly" ×8;
  - the criminal-structure line: "keeps the criminal operation suppressed rather than eliminated" ×6;
  - the faith and defence annex lines ×3 each.
- **The retired word prints about 25 times in every dossier.** That is 298 in 12. It sits in headings ("The Factions (3)"), in the annex ("How the powers and factions stand to one another") and in hooks ("Both factions have received the offer"). The largest source counts are:
  - `power.generated.js` 71, `npcGenerator.js` 71, `factionDynamics.js` 63, `general.generated.js` 37;
  - `stressInstitutionEffects.js` 19, `narrativeText.js` 18, `causalProse` 18, `stressFactions.js` 16.
- **A heading such as "Factions" is a label (law §4).** Renaming it is a word change on a label, so it is the owner's call or the chair's, not a style rewrite. The walker counts it all the same.


**The 61 printed "rather than", by writer file** (each printed sentence is attributed by the longest fragment around the phrase that occurs in a `src/` file; PLAUSIBLE where two files carry the same text):

| writer | printed in the 12 | on STYLE-0's list? |
|---|---|---|
| `src/generators/power/factionStanding.js` | 14 | no (this manifest: A4) |
| `src/data/npcData.js` | 13 | yes (identity text; audited for machinery only) |
| `src/data/dossierStateProse/defense.generated.js` | 7 | yes (W2's annex) |
| `src/data/institutionDescVariants.js` (its byte copy in `institutionDescColour.js`) | 6 | no (A2) |
| `src/generators/npcGenerator.js` | 5 | no (A7: its pools sit inside code the matcher misparsed) |
| `src/domain/summary/tonightSheet.js` | 3 | yes (W3/W4: the CAST_WANTS "thanked rather than blamed") |
| `src/data/dossierStateProse/economy.generated.js` | 3 | yes (W1) |
| `src/data/cultureProfiles.js` | 3 | yes (identity text) |
| `src/data/dossierStateProse/power.generated.js` | 3 | yes (W3) |
| `src/data/institutionalCatalog.js` | 3 | no (A2) |
| `src/generators/safetyProfile.js` | 1 | yes (W2) |

So 31 of the 61 come from writers the first wave never had, and 30 from STYLE-0 writers whose "rather than" lines the wave kept or wrote new. The biggest single sources are NPC personality text (npcData "interest rather than interrogation" ×10) and the power standing lines (factionStanding "riders to deals rather than fight directly" ×8).

**The walker must not inherit the census's JSX limit:** it scans JSX TEXT NODES (the `>text<` runs of every `.jsx` under its roots, `{…}` read as a slot) as well as string literals, and counts them in the same baseline.

## §4 The exclusions, with reasons (109 files, 1,363 literals; each opened, and each confirmed or reversed by its auditor — see §5)

Classes:
- **N1** — a diagnostic or generation-trace line that never reaches the dossier's prose. Three of these files the brief names; the 15 step files' `recordTrace` reasons feed the PipelineRail and AI grounding, the same class as `stepMetadata`.
- **N2** — code, regex, doc or comment fragments the matcher caught.
- **N3** — marketing, account, home or generate-screen copy outside any dossier.
- **N4** — the AI prompt.
- **N5** — a key table whose texts are byte copies of another file's.
- **N6** — thrown developer, validation or invariant errors.
- **N7** — Compendium-authoring and admin status lines.
- **N8** — editor, AI-overlay, export-menu and Foundry-install chrome.

**CONFIRMED.** Each item below was measured or read at `b7e32deed` by this seat:

- the 12-town soak (exit 0, 0 errors) and the `lit` flags, which are a lower bound by construction;
- §1-R: every deferred row's OLD text grepped in `src/`, and the seven W3 commits' ancestry;
- that R1–R5 print in the 12;
- §3's source and printed counts and the attribution of the 61;
- §4a's figures;
- the mechanical check of all 6,386 rows (§0);
- the 16-row hand sample and its one corrected clause;
- the `reformed`-stage exclusion (read in `causeLifecycle.js` and `npcComponents.jsx`);
- the inherited `tree` red and its commit.

**PLAUSIBLE:**
- **the per-row verdicts, codes, F/S tags, `needs` and NEW of §2.** Each is one auditor's judgment against its writer, checked two ways as above. The hand rate (one added clause in 16) means **a lane still reads every NEW against its writer's READS before taking it** (the census law);
- **the file-level exclusions that rest on a traced surface rather than on a mechanical fact:**
  - the 15 generation-trace step files (N1);
  - `activeConditions.js` (no surface prints `condition.description`);
  - `npcProfile.js`, `explanation.js`, `dailyLife.js`, `districtProfile.js`, `demographicReading.js`, `settlementQuickGuide.js`, `characterReadModel.js` (no production caller);
  - `SummaryTab.jsx`, `HousesBlock.jsx`, `WatchPanel.jsx`, `EngineSections.jsx` (unmounted);
  - `causeConjunctionClassContent.js` (a fallback that a full role table never reaches);
  - the 23 `newsVoice` floor lines;
  - the 34 catalogue ceiling reasons;
- **R1–R4's proposed fragments.** They are record-carried, so they move the generator golden master and the cast's sibling keys;
- **that `DailyLifeTab.jsx:341-348` prints only in local mode.**

Then the rest of `src/domain/worldPulse` (2,923 literals in 205 files) and `src/components/map` (305 in 42) that prints to a reader.
